                // ad loading js
                function setBackground(data) { 

                    var backgroundDiv = document.body; 
                    backgroundDiv.style.backgroundImage ="url('" + data.background_url + "')"; 
                    backgroundDiv.style.backgroundRepeat ="no-repeat"; 
                    backgroundDiv.style.backgroundPosition = "center 0px";

                    var backgroundLayer = document.getElementById("skin-dfp"); 
                    backgroundLayer.setAttribute('href', data.click_tracker); 
                    backgroundLayer.setAttribute('target', '_blank'); 

                    var impresion_DFP = document.createElement( 'img' );  
                    impresion_DFP.setAttribute('src', decodeURIComponent(data.impressionTracker));  
                    impresion_DFP.setAttribute('id', 'ImpressionTracker');  
                    impresion_DFP.style.top = "-1000px";
                    impresion_DFP.style.position = "fixed";
                    backgroundDiv.appendChild(impresion_DFP);  

                    var impresion_thirdParty = document.createElement( 'img' );  
                    impresion_thirdParty.setAttribute('src', data.thirdPartyImpressionTracker);  
                    impresion_thirdParty.setAttribute('id', 'ThirdPartyImpressionTracker'); 
                    impresion_thirdParty.style.top = "-1000px";
                    impresion_thirdParty.style.position = "fixed";
                    backgroundDiv.appendChild(impresion_thirdParty);
                }

                document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
                    var active = false;
                    var lazyAds = lazyLoadedAds;
                    
                    // asynchronous function to refresh an ad unit every 30 seconds
                    async function refreshAdUnit(adUnit){
                        setInterval(function(){
                            var adSlot = document.getElementById(adUnit.getSlotElementId());
                            
                            if (adSlot && (adSlot.parentElement.getBoundingClientRect().top <= (window.innerHeight - 300)) && (adSlot.parentElement.getBoundingClientRect().top >= -100)){
                                googletag.cmd.push(function() {
                                    googletag.pubads().refresh([adUnit]);
                                });
                            }
                        }, 30000);
                    }

                    // call all sharethrough ads at once
                    googletag.cmd.push(function() {
                        googletag.pubads().refresh(sharethroughAds);
                    });
                    
                    // call lazy-loaded ads individually as they appear
                    const lazyLoad = function() {
                        if (active === false) {
                            active = true;

                            setTimeout(function() {
                                lazyAds.forEach(function(lazyAd) {
                                    var adSlot = document.getElementById(lazyAd.getSlotElementId());
                                    
                                    if (adSlot && (adSlot.parentElement.getBoundingClientRect().top <= (window.innerHeight + 300) && adSlot.parentElement.getBoundingClientRect().bottom >= 0) && getComputedStyle(adSlot.parentElement).display !== "none") {
                                        console.log(lazyAd.getSlotElementId());
                                        googletag.cmd.push(function() {
                                            googletag.pubads().refresh([lazyAd]);
                                        });

                                        if (refreshAds.includes(lazyAd)){
                                            refreshAdUnit(lazyAd);
                                        }

                                        lazyAds = lazyAds.filter(function(ad) {
                                            return ad !== lazyAd;
                                        });

                                        if (lazyAds.length === 0) {
                                            document.removeEventListener("scroll", lazyLoad);
                                            window.removeEventListener("resize", lazyLoad);
                                            window.removeEventListener("orientationchange", lazyLoad);
                                        }
                                    }
                                });

                                active = false;
                            }, 200);
                        }
                    };

                    document.addEventListener("scroll", lazyLoad);
                    window.addEventListener("resize", lazyLoad);
                    window.addEventListener("orientationchange", lazyLoad);
                });
            </script>

			//$('.cbp-spmenu .main-menu .submenu').css("display", "block");
			$('.cbp-spmenu .main-menu16 .submenu').toggle();
			if ($(this).hasClass('right')) {
				$(this).removeClass('right');
			} else {
				$(this).addClass('right');
			}
		});
		$('.cbp-spmenu .current.main-menu17 .arv').on('click', function () {
			/*$parent_box = $(this).closest('.arv');
			$parent_box.siblings().find('.submenu').slideUp();
			$parent_box.find('.submenu').slideToggle(1000, 'swing');*/
			//$('.cbp-spmenu .main-menu .submenu').css("display", "block");
			$('.cbp-spmenu .main-menu17 .submenu').toggle();
			if ($(this).hasClass('right')) {
				$(this).removeClass('right');
			} else {
				$(this).addClass('right');
			}
		});
	});

</script><link href="/assets/css/app.min.css" media="screen" rel="stylesheet" target="_parent" type="text/css"/>
